## Intentional deltas (document forever)

| Topic | Haxe | Rust | Why OK |
|-------|------|------|--------|
| Threading | per-conn threads + mutexes | intents + single writer | correctness |
| Save format | legacy bins | OLW1/OLN1/OLA1 (+ OLW **version 3** recursive NestedHelper) | fast restart |
| Nested containers on disk | recursive full ObjectHelper | **OLW3** NestedHelper + wire one colon level | map nest DONE; player body NestedHelper core DONE (Players.bin/PU residual) |
| AI | full AiBase | thin goals + **priority ladder** + **farmer/smith pure SM** + growth | incremental; live job ticks open |
| Commands | many bare words | `/` `!` prefix (except documented) | client strip + speech |
| Animals as entities | map ObjectHelpers | `AnimalWorld` + map mirror | same MX/combat outcomes |
| Animal pop/die | ObjectHelper + currentObjectsCount | entity counts + `original_counts` at seed | same chance gates; no groundObject cargo |
| Domestic dying factor | expression not assigned (Haxe bug) | no-op match | port-as-is |
| Tile insulation | rValue + isWall | map-slice + long-term | done TIME-WORLD-POLISH |
| groundObject persist | ObjectHelper disk | **OLW3** `ground_id` | done |
| Contained timers | ObjectHelper fields on disk | runtime `contained_timers` + NestedHelper slots (OLW3) | re-arm + creation clamp + slot uses; map-slice writes times+uses |
| Move speed extras | shoes/hitpoints/temp/grave/AI class | floor/road/biome + held/contain/boat + path gate | remaining factors separate |
| Chat mute | distance-only | `MuteBook` + DEAF | intentional product |
| Loved-food extra | say + PE happy | private PS + PE | DONE TH-MULTI-POLISH |
| Coin residual no kids | Haxe silent drop (TODO grave) | `ComplexObject.coins` on grave, else treasury | GPI-DEATH-POLISH implements Haxe TODO |
| Account id on grave | PlayerAccount.id | FNV-ish `account_soul_token(email)` | OLA1 has no numeric account id |
| Postload living owners | LoadPlayers=false prunes all | cold boot with empty players **keeps** disk owners | no player persist; session p_ids |
| Smith prepare order | steel block before iron textually | iron stock (stage&lt;3) before steel | stage labels ore→wrought→steel; Haxe iron nearly dead |
| Settings file format | `ServerSettings.txt` RTTI lines | `server.toml` TOML | same live knobs; mtime skip when unchanged |
| Settings default lines | `**default**` prefix skip | full TOML is active config | intentional |
| Season duration mid-reload | keeps `TimeToNextSeasonInYears` until roll | live apply sets current `season_length` + base; roll re-samples | ops-friendly; next roll uses Haxe formula |
| Season roll length | `SeasonDuration/2 + r·SeasonDuration` × hardness | `reseed_season_length_after_roll` (same formula) | hardness Winter/Summer square; Spring inherit skipped |
| Player heat ambient | full updateTemperature clothing/heat-obj/places | tile_temps + body_heat_step + clothing slot stub; live vitals/HX/move | MAP-TEMP-PLAYER core DONE; residual ambient branches deferred |
| Lockpick knobs | ServerSettings statics | LiveSettings + SimState.lockpick_settings | LOCKPICK-SETTINGS DONE |
| Bloody weapon TTC | content timeTransition (−1,newActor) × cool-down factor | base 2s × 0.5/5; makeWeapon fixed 3s | content table residual |
| Ally strength pickup gate | AllyStrenghTooLowForPickup (default 0) | pure + USE `use_transition` wire; const default 0 (off) | not yet LiveSettings knob; path ready when >0 |
| Breastfeed restore factor | FoodRestoreFactorWhileFeeding=10 | same in `feed.rs` | BREASTFEED-EDGES |
| Max child feeding | Math.max(4, food_store_max) | `get_max_child_feeding` | not 0.85×food_max |
